Result: 与现有基线相比，方法在MAE上降低62.01%，并在RMSE上取得一致的改进；发布了新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。

Conclusion: 深度信息与多尺度交叉注意力结合可显著提升拥挤遮挡场景下的视频目标计数准确性，统一去重框架有效解决跨帧冗余问题，新数据集为后续研究提供支持。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [102] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个无需GAN的流匹配框架，用于非配对图像到图像翻译。它通过学习每个特征的门控机制替代传统的全局噪声/引导控制，该门控基于源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离，同时控制初始化和ODE传输时机。门控可局部覆盖，在五个自然与生物医学基准上取得最优或接近最优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非配对图像翻译方法使用单一的全局噪声或引导值来控制图像变换过程中的内容保留，无法区分需要保留的内容与需要改变的视觉外观。这导致结构保留与外观变换之间的平衡难以精确控制，尤其在医学图像等领域需要精准保留语义结构的场景下表现不佳。

Method: PRISM采用免GAN的流匹配框架，核心是学习每个特征的门控（per-feature gate）。门控的空间先验由各源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离导出：远离目标分布的特征被释放变换，与目标一致的特征被保留。同一门控同时控制两个环节：(1)初始化阶段，将真实源潜在表示与任务匹配的噪声混合（结构保留任务用内容锚定的AdaIN，结构变化任务用部分锚定）；(2)ODE积分过程中的传输时机。门控可在推理时通过文本或检测器进行局部覆盖而无需重新训练。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ猫→狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、白天→夜晚重打光、虚拟染色、乳腺冷冻→永久组织病理学）上，在相同数据划分协议下，PRISM在四个基准取得最佳Inception FID和KID，在第五个基准取得有竞争力的结果；在组织病理学上核计数比最接近理想值，表明在目标真实感与结构保留之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: PRISM通过学习逐特征门控替代全局控制，有效解耦了非配对图像翻译中"保留什么"与"改变什么"的问题，在不同领域（自然图像和生物医学）均验证了方法的有效性，尤其展示了在需要严格结构保留的医学应用中优越的性能。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [103] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38数据集（10,874张专家验证的孟加拉手语图像，覆盖38种手势）及一个仅298,470参数的轻量级注意力卷积网络，在精确匹配协议下达到96.37%准确率（五种子平均95.72±0.54%），与ImageNet预训练架构差距仅1.08个百分点，参数量减少8.5-68倍，MACs减少1.3-21.7倍；量化后仅0.48MB，在智能手机上15.5MB内存占用下每张图像处理3.98ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋哑人主要通过BdSL交流，但现有自动识别系统使用无专家验证的受控数据集，且依赖不适合端侧部署的重型预训练骨干网络，难以在个人设备上实现无障碍访问教育和服务。

Method: 构建RSBdSL38数据集（来自孟加拉三所特教学校真实手语者的10,874张专家验证图像，覆盖孟加拉字母51个字母代表的38种手势），并提出轻量级注意力卷积网络：由分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化及Swish激活构成，从零训练。

Result: 达到96.37%准确率（五种子95.72±0.54%），与九个ImageNet预训练高效架构中最优者仅差1.08个百分点；六个公开BdSL基准上重训练达92.95-98.33%，合并语料库97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%；移除任何架构阶段损失7.61-89.30点（训练策略最多3.17点）；留出6/36个手语者的签名者独立划分达85.18%；量化后0.48MB，智能手机上3.98ms/图。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集与从零训练的轻量模型将基准准确率转化为可部署的无障碍解决方案，成本仅为预训练骨干网络的一小部分；Grad-CAM及删除-插入、权重随机化检验确认预测确实跟随签名手势；数据集、代码和模型均已发布。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [104] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS（Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State），一种将世界状态与视图渲染显式解耦的多玩家视频世界模型。通过学习的逻辑引擎从联合动作推进全局共享状态，再通过渲染引擎按需生成任意相机的独立一致视图，解决了传统多玩家环境模型中状态纠缠、计算冗余和视图不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多玩家环境中表现不佳，因为其将世界状态与视角相关的视觉潜在表示纠缠在一起，导致：(1) 冗余计算——每个视角都重复建模共享的世界状态；(2) 视图不一致——不同视角生成的画面彼此矛盾；(3) 可扩展性差——随着玩家数量增长难以维持。受多玩家游戏架构启发，作者希望将世界动力学和视图渲染解耦，实现可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟。

Method: MAS的核心架构包含两个关键组件：1) 逻辑引擎（Logic Engine）——学习将全局权威类型化状态从联合动作中推进，无需任何手写转移函数，作为唯一的循环记忆和同步参考；2) 渲染引擎（Rendering Engine）——从共享状态按需为任意请求的摄像机生成独立且一致的视图。两个引擎均为可学习模型，通过显式解耦实现世界状态与视图渲染的分离。

Result: 在匹配的多玩家Snake基准上与最先进的多视图基线相比，MAS实现了更优越的状态精度和更低的跨视图不一致性。能够支持1,024个并发玩家推进预测世界10,000个循环步骤。

Conclusion: 显式的权威共享状态建模为可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础，验证了将世界动力学与视图渲染解耦的有效性。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [105] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OTLesMix，一种基于Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划的图像合成方法，用于生成多样化的脑病变分割训练样本，在三个脑病变分割任务上将Dice分数提升2.9至6.6个百分点，并优于现有基于混合的数据增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合策略的数据增强方法生成的新样本在病变形状和位置方面存在有限的可变性，限制了其在医学图像分割中的增强效果。

Method: 利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划来生成逼真且多样化的合成样本，命名为OTLesMix。

Result: 在三个脑病变分割任务上，相比不采用合成数据训练的模型，Dice分数提升2.9至6.6个百分点，并优于最先进的基于混合的方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix通过最优传输理论生成多样且逼真的合成医学图像，有效增强了脑病变分割模型的训练性能。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [106] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号模型，使用定制化的Mask R-CNN管道在1,272张标注图像上训练，通过掩膜灰度世界白平衡算法和生理约束检测层来应对患者自产生数据的变异性，内部测试和外部测试均表现良好，并作为开源容器化API提供。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人面临口腔健康问题，但专业牙科护理的成本和可及性限制了预防性口腔保健。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口内摄像头图像，普通公众无法获取这些设备进行自我筛查，因此需要一种基于日常智能手机照片的自动口腔健康筛查方法。

Method: 开发了一个定制化的Mask R-CNN管道，在1,272张标注的智能手机图像上训练。管道包含两个领域知识驱动的机制：(1) 掩膜灰度世界白平衡算法，用于缓解人工颜色偏移；(2) 解剖学约束的检测层，强制结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估分为四个阶段：内部留出测试、独立外部测试、描述性消融研究和基于折的训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上，模型达到实例掩膜AP@50为0.818、类别感知PQ为0.780、操作F1为0.884。训练稳定性良好，十次运行中AP@50标准差为0.009。外部数据集上，尽管人群、传感器和采集协议存在差异，模型仍达到AP@50为0.901、PQ为0.832、F1为0.928。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像能够支持自动化的牙齿级解剖映射，为资源有限环境中的远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展、低成本的基础。推理管道已作为开源、容器化API发布。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [107] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HoloCount，一个基于三级层级分类法的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）视觉计数综合评测基准，涵盖语义计数、分析计数和鲁棒性测试三大维度，通过对20余个先进MLLM的系统评估，揭示了模型从感知到复杂推理及对抗场景下性能显著退化的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉计数基准仅关注简化情境下的基础感知任务，无法捕捉逻辑约束或对抗条件下出现的复杂失效模式；同时MLLMs在定量精度上存在显著的数值幻觉瓶颈，需要更全面、更具诊断性的评测体系来系统刻画其计数能力。

Method: HoloCount采用三层级分类法：(1) 语义计数——原子级和基于属性（property-based）的枚举；(2) 分析计数——通过空间和集合推理进行逻辑组合评估；(3) 鲁棒性测试——针对高密度场景和语言偏见等不利情境及"接地反先验"（grounded counter-priors）探测模型完整性。数据集已在 https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/ 公开。

Result: 对20多个最先进的MLLMs进行详尽评估后，揭示了一个关键性能差距：即便是顶级模型，当任务从感知转向复杂分析推理和对抗场景时，性能也会显著下降，表明当前MLLMs在定量精确计数方面仍存在系统性不足。

Conclusion: HoloCount系统性地刻画了当前MLLMs的计数能力全貌，为开发更可靠、更具接地性的多模态系统提供了路线图，是推动视觉计数从感知走向深层推理的重要评测资源。

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.

</details>


### [108] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc扩展了LightLoc架构，通过各向异性高斯协方差头预测每个体素的全3x3正定协方差矩阵，并使用NLL损失和kNN空间平滑正则化训练，从而在LiDAR场景坐标回归中实现了不确定性量化，同时提升了6-DoF定位精度并产生校准良好的协方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR场景坐标回归方法产生确定性预测，丢弃了随机（aleatoric）不确定性信息，而这种不确定性捕获本可以提升系统鲁棒性并支持后续决策制定。作者旨在填补这一空白，为SCR方法引入不确定性量化能力。

Method: 1) 在LightLoc架构上扩展一个各向异性高斯协方差头，预测每个体素的全3x3正定协方差矩阵；2) 使用负对数似然（NLL）损失并结合基于kNN的空间平滑正则化器进行训练；3) 推理阶段采用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，包含基于不确定性的加权种子评分和基于马氏距离的内点检验。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上取得了一致的改进，同时产生了校准良好的协方差预测，并使用期望校准误差（ECE）作为评估预测不确定度质量的原则性指标。

Conclusion: UQ-Loc证明了将不确定性量化集成到LiDAR场景坐标回归中不仅不会损害定位精度，反而能通过概率建模和不确定性感知的求解策略实现精度与校准性的双重提升。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [109] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了超分辨率（SR）重建是否会影响白质高信号（WMH）分割的病灶内容完整性。基于ADNI队列29例受试者的1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描，模拟3mm和5mm厚层采集并通过三种SR方法（多对比度隐式神经表示INR、单对比度自监督ECLARE、三次插值）重建，以最敏感的MARS-WMH分割评估。结果表明SR的主要效应是抹除小真实病灶而非幻觉生成，且随层厚增加而加剧；ECLARE在两种层厚下对小病灶信号恢复最佳，INR与三次插值相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床FLAIR扫描常以厚层采集导致面内分辨率差，SR常用于恢复各向同性体积，但其在WMH分割前应用是保留还是破坏病灶内容（可能抹除小真实病灶或幻觉出不存在病灶）此前未知，该研究旨在填补这一知识空白。

Method: 使用ADNI队列29例1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描（专家手动分割WMH），将数据退化模拟3mm与5mm厚层采集；采用多对比度INR、单对比度自监督ECLARE和三次插值上采样至HR网格；在四种WMH分割方法中选择对HR小病灶最敏感的MARS-WMH进行逐病灶分析，采用检测灵敏度、抹除率（HR检测病灶在重建后丢失）和幻觉率（预测成分在手动与HR分割中均不存在）作为评价指标。

Result: SR的主要效应是抹除小真实病灶而非幻觉生成，且该抹除效应随切片厚度增大而加剧；然而所有重建方法相比原始厚层扫描均改善了病灶检测。ECLARE在3mm和5mm两种厚度下对小病灶信号恢复最佳，而INR的表现不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: SR应用于WMH分割前存在病灶抹除风险，尤其对小病灶和厚层扫描；ECLARE是最佳重建方法，而INR和插值方法需谨慎使用；研究建议在临床实践中重视SR对小型病灶的潜在影响，并为未来SR方法设计提供病灶保持评估框架。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting——一种多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体并组织成层级嵌套结构，通过"应用即智能体"的集成范式和"对话即集成"的交互理念，实现跨异构系统的自然语言交互与自动化编排，克服传统ESB、API网关和RPA集成方案的耦合度高、运维成本高、智能性不足等局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业广泛依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效编排和利用已投入大量资源建设的企业应用。

Method: 提出分层嵌套的多智能体协作框架：从每个遗留应用提取数字代理智能体以支持自然语言交互和自主操作；通过中央编排器协调多智能体进行任务分解和动态调度；暴露统一对话界面用于跨应用查询和流程编排。与平面互连不同，智能体按层级管理模式组织，映射企业生态系统的组合复杂性。

Result: 论文的主要贡献在于提出了"Application-as-Agent"（应用即智能体）集成范式与"Conversation-as-Integration"（对话即集成）交互理念，并在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中探索了该方法的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting框架为企业应用集成开辟了新的智能agent协作路径，有望解决数据孤岛与流程碎片化问题，为大规模异构系统协调提供可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [111] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出「点火指数」（Ignition Index, I），一个从全局工作空间理论（GWT）出发、用于量化变压器语言模型中「全有或全无」点火预测的标量指标。该指标通过对各层线性探针准确率进行四参数Sigmoid拟合，提取陡峭参数β̂来衡量相变剧烈程度。在11个模型、5种架构族中验证了9.6倍选择性识别真实语言结构，并发现前馈变压器点火指数比SSM高89%，循环架构在迭代维度上呈现点火现象，以及训练早期存在阶段转变等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球工作空间理论（GWT）预测认知系统的信息处理呈现「全有或全无」的点火式相变，但此前缺乏一种经验性、可验证的指标来将这一理论预测与神经网络的机制可解释性研究桥接起来。现有研究多依赖定性观察，缺乏定量度量来识别和比较不同架构中的点火行为。

Method: 该方法对每个Transformer层的线性探针准确率随输入信号强度变化的曲线拟合一个四参数Sigmoid函数，提取其中的陡峭参数β̂作为点火指数。通过打乱标签的控制实验验证指标的选择性（9.6倍），再对11个涵盖5种架构族的模型进行系统测量，并用Mann-Whitney U检验、Cohen's d、PELT断点检测等统计工具分析结果。

Result: （1）前馈Transformer的聚合β̂比SSM高89%（p<1e-13，d=0.52），Mamba呈近线性轮廓，缺乏全局广播；（2）Huginn-3.5B在迭代轴上的点火强度是深度轴的2.12倍，表明循环架构沿循环维度展现工作空间式转变；（3）Pythia-410M在第256步训练时出现PELT检测到的阶段转变（+67%），早于归纳头的形成；（4）点火与模型规模、信号强度的假设未获证实，暗示变压器架构可能已饱和现有点火机制。

Conclusion: Ignition Index是首个经验证的、将GWT动态预测与机制可解释性定量连接起来的指标，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和此前缩放定律文献中未表征的架构级区分能力，为理解不同神经网络架构中的信息整合与全局广播机制提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [112] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation，一种弱到强的训练框架，通过对比局部干预（成功与失败的弱模型补丁）构造校正性的教师分布，并将其蒸馏到强模型中，从而修复推理过程中的局部错误，提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，但这些失败并非源于模型整体能力不足，而是中间步骤中的局部推理错误。作者发现这些错误可以通过插入弱模型生成的短补丁来修复，但直接微调模型去学习这些补丁并不能有效内化校正能力，说明有用的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于干预如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation框架：在相同的推理前缀位置，对比成功和失败的弱模型补丁，构造一个校正性的教师分布（基于它们诱导的未来token预测），然后将这一信号蒸馏到强模型中，从而让强模型学会自我校正而不依赖外部干预。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation一致地提升强模型性能，并优于直接模仿（直接微调于弱补丁或修复轨迹）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是可修复的，而有效的学习信号来自对比性局部干预如何重塑推理分布，而非干预文本本身；Woodpecker Distillation通过蒸馏这种校正性分布，成功地将这一能力内化到强模型中。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [113] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中，是否可以将连续型预测因子替换为基于临床指南的类别型编码而不牺牲模型性能。在三个治疗队列中，完全类别化的梯度提升模型在两个队列中与连续模型统计上无差异，特征重要性排序保持一致，表明基于指南的离散化是可行的模型设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中已达到较高预测精度，但临床采纳受限，因为模型解释与临床医生推理方式不一致。医生用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，因此研究者希望探明是否能在不牺牲性能的前提下用临床信息化的类别编码替代连续预测因子。

Method: 使用多中心欧洲登记数据，将患者分为三个治疗队列。比较标准连续型梯度提升模型与完全类别化梯度提升模型，后者采用卒中指南对齐的、按治疗特异的阈值进行离散化。通过统计检验比较两类模型的预测准确性，并分析全局特征重要性排序是否保持一致。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全类别化模型在其中两个队列中与连续型模型在统计上无法区分；在另一个队列中出现显著的预测准确性下降。两类模型的全局特征重要性排序一致，表明离散化保留了所有治疗组中预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的类别化编码是卒中结局预测模型可行的设计选择，能够在保持性能的同时使模型解释与临床医生推理方式对齐，从而可能提升临床采纳度。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [114] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个针对LLM智能体技能复用场景的多轨迹溯源审计框架，从表达、实现和操作三条轨迹提取证据，构建技能操作图（SOG）表示操作结构，在审计时采用确定性比较并逐轨迹校准，在包含820个正样本和751个负样本的基准上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898，并在36,446个技能的大规模审计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体生态中可复用技能（包含元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具、引用和工作流的多模态包）正成为市场商品，但现有检测方法仅针对单模态源码或整体包相似性，无法捕获仅保留技能某个部分（如仅操作结构或仅实现片段）的复用行为，需要一种能跨模态追踪技能复用证据的审计框架。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三种溯源轨迹：表达轨迹（Expression）、实现轨迹（Implementation）和操作轨迹（Operational）。其中操作轨迹被建模为技能操作图（SOG），捕获激活、流程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取阶段辅助一次操作轨迹提取，审计时对缓存的轨迹进行确定性比较，针对同功能严格负样本校准每条轨迹，并报告哪些轨迹支持复用判定。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上（820个转换后的复用正样本覆盖100个市场锚点技能，751个负对照），SKILLTRACE实现AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。在36,446个技能的真实世界审计中，基于轨迹归因的证据能够产生出可行动作复用审查队列，超越仓库级基线。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多轨迹溯源与确定性审计机制，有效解决了LLM智能体技能市场中部分复用的检测难题，证明了跨模态轨迹提取与归因分析在技能复用审计中的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [115] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），一种关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为泛化的抽象因果逻辑，以解决实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上的泛化缺陷；并设计了多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统提取可信AECR，构建两个知识库，以及一个抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE）将其注入事件预测任务，实验证明能显著提升事件因果推理的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心智能分析系统依赖显式因果事件知识，但实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，无法有效支持风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解等下游任务。

Method: 提出AECR将具体因果对抽象为关系级因果逻辑；构建多智能体CACI系统并结合相似度约束聚类，从含噪原始因果数据中提取可信AECR；在此基础上提出AR-GCAE编码器，通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入CGEP基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR能显著增强事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，尤其在稀有和未见事件样本上增益最为突出。

Conclusion: 抽象因果规则知识能够有效弥补实例级因果知识的泛化不足，为事件中心智能分析系统的因果推理能力提供可靠支撑。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [116] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个仅15.3M参数的人类国际象棋AI，通过将棋局建模为时间感知的顺序过程（而非孤立地看待每个局面），利用最近20步历史与时钟压力两个条件信号，在6.1亿个位置上训练，实现了55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5人类走子预测准确率，超越此前最优模型Maia 2，且参数更少、训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的人类国际象棋走子预测模型（如Maia 2）主要将每个局面视为独立输入，忽略了棋局的时间性与序列性特征。作者认为人类决策实际上受开局偏好、局内行为趋势和时钟压力等时间相关因素的影响，因此需要建模为时间感知的序列过程，以实现更准确的人类走子预测。

Method: Otter采用两个条件信号：（1）走子历史编码器，基于最近20步棋来调节预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向；（2）时间控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型在117百万局Lichess快棋赛的61亿个位置上训练，仅使用单块T4 GPU训练30天。

Result: Otter在top-1准确率55.23%和top-5准确率90.95%上超越此前SOTA人类棋类模型Maia 2。在11个Elo分段（<1100到>=2000）中，准确率在1900-1999分段达到峰值57.38%。相较Maia 2，参数量更少，训练数据也更少。

Conclusion: 将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动比仅基于局面的方法能产生更接近人类走子行为的预测，且可以使用更小的模型实现。代码、训练模型和完整训练日志已公开。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [117] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准和SearchAuditor框架，用于评估和提升LLM审计器定位、归因和修复深度搜索智能体失败轨迹的能力。实验表明SearchAuditor在端到端通过率上超越所有基线（32.3% vs 最强基线26.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体通过长时间跨度的网页交互处理复杂问题，但该过程脆弱：小推理错误可能在长而嘈杂的轨迹中传播成流畅但错误的答案。诊断此类失败需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，亟需自动化审计工具来减少人工负担。

Method: 1）构建SearchAuditBench：包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K token），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的运行，每条轨迹有专家标注的关键错误步骤、搜索特定的根本原因和带评分标准的参考修复方案。2）提出SearchAuditor：一种多视角审计框架，通过证据基础的裁决来有效定位、归因和修复搜索智能体失败。

Result: 即使最强基线配合GPT-5.5等前沿模型，端到端通过率仅达26.6%；SearchAuditor在多个前沿模型上一致超越所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，且利用其修复方案恢复失败运行可帮助智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditor能够有效减少审计深度搜索智能体失败的人工负担，通过自动化定位、归因和修复错误来帮助智能体恢复，为深度搜索智能体的可靠性提升提供了新途径。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [118] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS是一种基于3D高斯溅射的说话人头像生成方法，通过引入时间对齐的音素令牌和语言融合模块，解决说话头像中口型生成的"漏嘴"伪影和过平滑问题，在HDTF数据集上取得最佳唇部几何精度（LMD 2.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话头像虽然能实现快速逼真的渲染，但嘴唇发音精度不足：口型常被过度平滑，且可能违反双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。根本原因是将短暂、离散的发音事件从连续声学嵌入中通过回归目标推断，导致预测偏向平均化的口型配置。现代自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和音素线索，但缺乏显式的、逐帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠地区分闭合级事件。

Method: PD-GS的核心方法包括：(1) 利用自动语音识别和强制对齐管线获取时间对齐的音素令牌，与连续音频特征共同驱动3DGS说话人模型；(2) 设计语言融合模块（LFM），通过可学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保留平滑音频驱动动态的同时加强对发音关键片段的音素引导；(3) 仅使用单目视频进行训练，通过图像重建和唇部地标监督优化模型。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇部几何精度上优于所有对比基线（LMD降至2.66），并在挑战性音素序列中显著减少闭合违规，生成更具语言学保真度的神经头像。

Conclusion: 通过将离散音素令牌与连续音频特征通过门控机制融合，PD-GS有效缓解了3DGS说话头像中口型过平滑和"漏嘴"伪影问题，验证了显式语言学线索对提升唇部发音精度的重要性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [119] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，解决特权on-policy蒸馏在多轮交互环境中因状态-参考不匹配导致的监督信号不可靠问题。SMRC-SD在每轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态下进行蒸馏，并构建状态条件化的教师上下文。在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD显著优于无条件完整路径蒸馏，Qwen3-1.7B上任务成功率分别从0.746提升到0.865和从0.574提升到0.693。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互环境中，特权on-policy蒸馏允许同步教师利用训练时才有的参考（如成功轨迹）对学生的每轮响应重新评分以提供密集监督。然而学生先前的动作会持续改变执行状态，导致其rollout可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，使参考对该状态不可靠，盲目应用特权蒸馏会造成状态-参考不匹配（state-reference mismatch）。核心目标是为学生当前执行状态提供兼容的特权参考引导。

Method: 提出状态匹配路由与上下文化自蒸馏（State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation, SMRC-SD）。核心机制：1) 状态匹配路由：在每轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某个支持状态匹配，仅在匹配的状态下才应用蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容引导的轮次；2) 上下文化自蒸馏：对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹构建状态条件化的教师上下文，将监督基于实际到达的状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个环境上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功完整路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B时，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。受控的路由和上下文消融实验表明，选择局部支持的轮次和构建状态兼容的教师上下文均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式判断特权轨迹何时以及如何引导当前状态下的on-policy学生，有效解决了状态-参考不匹配问题，验证了"仅在匹配状态下蒸馏"和"基于实际状态构建教师上下文"这两个设计原则在多轮智能体训练中的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [120] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究了在人类行为数据上微调的大语言模型作为认知代理的规模效应，训练了从1.35亿到140亿参数的14个模型（4种架构族）在Psych-101数据集（160个实验、1070万次试次选择）。发现分布内（in-distribution）规模几乎不重要，所有模型表现接近天花板；分布外（out-of-distribution）则呈现明显更陡峭的扩展梯度，大模型在泛化到新任务结构时优势显著。通过剥离四种提示通道和置换试次顺序的诊断实验，发现遮盖刺激内容和反馈会破坏75.7%的学习信息并将模型推向低于机会水平，证明选择历史不足以解释性能。小型认知微调模型有望作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，但适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型在人类行为数据上微调后作为通用认知代理的可行性，并回答两个悬而未决的问题：（1）所需的模型规模有多大？（2）这些模型真正处理的是任务结构，还是仅仅利用统计捷径（如选择历史）？这关系到认知建模的可信度和可推广性。

Method: 作者在Psych-101数据集上训练了14个模型，参数量从135M到14B，覆盖4种架构族。通过对比分布内/分布外的扩展曲线来评估规模效应；通过系统性剥离四种提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）并置换试次顺序，来进行信息使用诊断分析。

Result: （1）分布内表现随规模变化极小，模型集中在窄带内，0.6B到1B参数即可匹配70B基线模型在留出参与者上的表现；（2）分布外扩展梯度显著更陡，大模型在新任务结构泛化上明显占优；（3）遮盖刺激内容和反馈使模型丢失75.7%的学习信息并降至低于机会水平，表明模型并非仅依赖选择历史；（4）置换试次顺序在独立试次任务上不敏感，但在试次顺序由先前反应决定的任务上表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: 小规模认知微调模型可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，具有成本效益优势；但其适用范围仍受限于训练中见到的实验范式，分布外泛化仍需更大模型来实现。选择历史不是主要的信息来源，模型确实利用了刺激和反馈信息来学习任务结构。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [121] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，用于将研究项目分解为有界、依赖感知的任务契约。通过创新原子和谱系图表示候选贡献，并使用轻量级伯努利块模型选择水平/垂直/混合组合分解。在10个项目简报基准上，平均质量分数7.15（对比基线4.58和5.31），并将下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单任务系统将研究项目视为一个过大任务，产生扁平、模糊或重叠的任务集合，且任务边界和执行顺序依赖人工协调。研究项目本质上是需通过多个有界子任务推进的长时程议程，包含不同但相关的目标、并行备选方案和依赖感知的执行序列，现有方法缺乏对这种项目级结构的显式建模。

Method: Project2Task包含三层机制：(1) 将候选贡献表示为"创新原子"，组织成有向谱系图；(2) 使用轻量级伯努利块模型目标优化，在水平、垂直和混合组合分解之间进行选择；(3) 生成带有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失执行字段，输出依赖感知的任务契约，涵盖目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。契约与具体研究执行器无关。

Result: 在包含10个项目简报、约30个任务的基准上：手稿级组合评估中，Project2Task平均质量分数为7.15，优于Brief Baseline的4.58和Topic-only Setting的5.31；与AutoResearchClaw集成后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、无冗余且可执行的研究任务组合至关重要，Project2Task验证了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [122] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriQua，一个基于"分解-验证"范式的LLM事实性评估框架，通过将简单声明提取为标准三元组、复杂声明表示为带上下文限定符的超关系事实，兼顾了原子性和上下文完整性；其配套的TriQuaScore评分机制与人工标注高度一致，在分解质量和证据基事实验证上优于现有框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"分解-验证"范式面临根本性权衡：原子事实虽粒度精确但常丢失必要上下文，而更宽泛的陈述又缺乏细粒度评估能力。需要一个能根据事实复杂度灵活建模的结构，在保持原子性的同时保留检索与验证所需的上下文。

Method: 提出TriQua框架：(1) 将简单声明提取为标准三元组（subject-predicate-object）；(2) 将复杂声明表示为超关系事实，通过附加辅助上下文限定符（qualifiers）来保留关键上下文；(3) 验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性；(4) 配套提出TriQuaScore用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性程度。

Result: 实验证明：TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；TriQua实现了稳健的分解质量；在基于证据的事实验证任务中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: 自适应的事实结构（简单声明用三元组、复杂声明用带限定符的超关系事实）能够有效解决原子性与上下文完整性之间的矛盾，为LLM事实性评估提供了更精确且可解释的方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [123] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统能将书面梦境描述转化为时间顺序的四格图像序列。系统先使用大语言模型将梦境描述划分为四个按时间顺序的部分，再利用文本到图像模型为每个部分生成图像，并通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL等模型进行客观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境往往情绪强烈但难以向他人传达，需要一种能够将文字梦境描述可视化呈现的系统，帮助人们更直观地分享和理解梦境内容。

Method: DSV系统包含两个核心阶段：首先利用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；随后使用文本到图像模型为每个部分生成图像，并通过再生机制确保序列中视觉连贯性。评估采用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型对质量、保真度和连贯性进行客观度量。

Result: 通过对DreamBank中梦境描述的50个可视化结果进行评估，报告了质量、保真度和连贯性等方面的客观指标结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统成功实现了将文字梦境描述转化为具有视觉连贯性的四格图像序列，为梦境可视化提供了可行方案，并通过多模态评估模型验证了生成效果。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [124] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Search2Skill框架，通过评分准则驱动的强化学习方案，自动识别LLM代理的能力缺口、搜索外部资源填补缺口、并将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，在八个专家级领域的基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化技能方法仅从模型参数知识或轨迹内部构建技能，受限于模型已有知识边界。但专业技能的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出该边界，难以仅靠代理自身引导出来，因此需要引入外部搜索来弥补能力缺口。

Method: Search2Skill框架包含三个核心环节：(1)自动识别代理的能力缺口；(2)搜索外部资源以填补这些缺口；(3)将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化、可复用的技能。整个框架通过基于评分准则(rubric)的强化学习方案进行优化，联合改进何时搜索、如何搜索以及如何生成技能。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式和保留式两种评估协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能提升源于技能的抽象化而非原始检索证据，且习得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill验证了通过外部搜索结合强化学习蒸馏技能的有效性，证明了技能抽象比原始检索证据更具价值，并展示了习得技能在不同规模模型间的可迁移性，为LLM代理在专家领域的自进化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [125] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUNAR是首个基于跨领域纵向应用行为历史（涵盖衣食住行等日常活动）来评估LLM个性化能力的基准，通过多阶段粗到细的合成管线构建，实验发现行为日志虽必要但不充分，证据选择、跨域整合与隐私控制是个性化LLM的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准依赖文本人格画像或孤立的单行为信号，缺乏对跨领域行为个性化的系统评估——即回答需要扎根于异构的日常活动历史。为了弥合这一空白，作者提出LUNAR基准。

Method: LUNAR采用多阶段粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的合成管线，基于真实行为模式构建纵向应用交互历史，覆盖服装、饮食、住房、出行四大日常领域，以缓解数据稀疏性和隐私问题，同时保证可扩展的基准构建。通过保真度分析验证合成数据与真实行为分布的接近程度，并在19个主流LLM上开展系统实验。

Result: (1) 保真度分析显示LUNAR比其它合成基准更贴近真实行为分布；(2) 行为日志访问对个性化是必要的但不充分——更多上下文或更大模型并不保证更好性能，取决于证据的选择与跨领域整合；(3) 直接检索细粒度行为记录优于压缩记忆；(4) 更强的个性化可能以牺牲隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 证据选择、跨领域整合和隐私控制是当前个性化LLM面临的核心挑战，LUNAR为系统评估和推动这一方向的研究提供了新基准。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [126] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的、从粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架，通过规划智能体将文本提示转换为结构化场景规格，先生成全局一致的地形基础，再为细节要求高的区域生成可编辑的纹理网格资产并恢复其在场景中的放置，最终生成具有全局空间连贯性、丰富局部内容且支持下游编辑的大型3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界面临的核心挑战是：系统需要同时维护全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容，以及适用于下游编辑和复用的显式资产。现有方法难以在同一框架内同时满足这三方面需求。

Method: WorldClaw采用完全智能体化（agentic）的从粗到细（coarse-to-fine）框架。规划智能体将文本提示分解为包含区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规格说明。之后构建全局连贯的地形基础（基于语义布局、可复用资产、生成式或程序化材质、区域感知高度场）。对于细节要求高的区域，生成地形条件化组合、重建可编辑的纹理网格，并恢复其在场景中的放置；渲染基智能体进一步优化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw生成了具有连贯空间组织、视觉吸引人的局部内容以及可编辑实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持了全局一致的地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw通过智能体化、从粗到细的分层生成策略，成功解决了开放世界3D场景生成中全局连贯性与局部丰富性、可编辑资产三者之间的矛盾，为大规模开放世界3D内容创作提供了一种可用的框架。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [127] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对联合行动规划中的"预承诺态势"（Pre-commitment posture）问题，即冲突场景未解决前将军事资产分配到战区位置的最优配置问题，提出了基于有限时域马尔可夫决策过程的场景加权对抗鲁棒态势优化引擎。核心贡献包括CEV优化器和其对抗扩展RobustCEV，后者迭代应对一个根据已观测部署更新攻击目标的贝叶斯对抗者。在包含20个资产和5个战区位置的印太基地环境中，实验证明贪心基线存在25.1%的永久态势效率损失和57.3%的场景加权战备度崩溃；CEV优化器在地理信号威胁分布下可恢复高达19.8%的效率；RobustCEV在面对采用欺骗性威胁先验的适应性对抗者时可恢复高达158%的效率。所有发现均通过配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机源于军事联合行动规划中的一个关键且尚未被正式解决的问题：预承诺态势（即在冲突场景明确之前将军事资产预先部署到战区位置）。当前实践依赖贪心启发式算法，这类算法仅最大化资产价值而忽略地理覆盖度，且结构性地容易受到针对高战略价值位置打击的对抗者攻击。这种脆弱性在真实军事场景中可能导致严重的战备度下降和态势效率损失，因此亟需一种更鲁棒的形式化求解方法。

Method: 本文方法包含三个核心部分：（1）将态势与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），以资产、战区位置和时间步为决策维度；（2）提出CEV（Composite Expected Value）优化器，通过在威胁场景分布上最大化场景加权的期望态势效率来分配资产；（3）提出RobustCEV扩展版本，通过迭代对抗一个贝叶斯对抗者（该对抗者根据观测到的资产部署动态更新其攻击目标分布）来增强鲁棒性。实验在包含20个资产和5个战区位置的印太基地环境中进行，涉及三种对比和验证方案。

Result: 实验结果包含三个主要发现：（1）贪心基线由于地理覆盖不足导致25.1%的永久性态势效率损失，且在价值相关对抗威胁下发生57.3%的场景加权战备度崩溃；（2）当威胁分布携带地理信号时，CEV优化器相对贪心基线可恢复高达19.8%的效率，且仅需5至20个精选场景即可捕获大部分增益；（3）当适应性对抗者采用欺骗性威胁先验时，RobustCEV扩展相对朴素优化器可恢复高达158%的效率。所有结果通过配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解验证，确认性能差距是部署策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影。

Conclusion: 本文结论表明，所提出的CEV优化器和RobustCEV扩展在军事资产态势分配问题上显著优于贪心基线和朴素优化器，尤其是在面对携带地理信号的威胁分布和采用欺骗性先验的适应性对抗者时。性能差距经过严格的统计验证，被确认为部署策略的结构性特征。该工作为联合行动规划中的预承诺态势问题提供了首个正式化的对抗鲁棒求解框架，兼具理论建模和实验验证的完备性。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [128] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench是一个面向多智能体编排系统的故障诊断基准框架，通过可控的种子可复现故障注入测试来评估失败、恢复与任务分解，引入了级联半径和分故障模式恢复两个核心指标，实验发现工具故障可完全恢复、模糊委托可部分恢复、潜在或语义故障完全不可恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体编排基准测试通常只报告任务准确率，而不诊断流水线失败的原因、级联故障的起始位置或具体哪个路由决策导致了故障，缺乏系统性的故障分析与归因能力。

Method: 使用受控的、种子可复现的故障注入框架，在模板化企业工作流上实施测试；引入级联半径（Cascade Radius）和分故障模式恢复率（Per-failure-mode Recovery）作为核心指标；采用bootstrap置信区间和配对检验比较路由策略；通过26例金标诊断集和可控机制探针（包括Claude代理在可验证的算术依赖链上的实验）评估故障处理层级；并进行了可信状态修复消融实验与盲重试对比。

Result: 关键词/标志路由器在具有误导性或缺失显式标志的对抗性案例上得分为0%，而意图推理模型路由器得分为100%（与oracle持平）。在五种MAST模式中识别出三级故障处理层级：工具故障完全恢复（1.0）、模糊委托部分恢复（0.30）、三类潜在或语义模式从未恢复（0.0）。级联半径随流水线深度从0.9增至4.7（深度3-7）。盲重试会重现潜在故障并增加检测时间。消融实验表明表面上的包含效果主要来源于可信状态信号而非自主检测。这些结果在贷款审批工作流和Sonnet/Opus/Haiku上保持一致的排序，尽管绝对数值受上下文影响。

Conclusion: 故障检测与归因是故障遏制的前提条件；路由器的语义推理能力对对抗性场景至关重要；潜在的/语义性故障模式是当前系统的主要薄弱点；研究结果属于受控链式机制探针而非领域工作负载声明，强调了基准测试需要从准确率报告转向故障诊断。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [129] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基准测试和日志追踪框架，用于评估不同代理架构（单代理、双代理、三代理拓扑）在显微控制任务中的表现，测试了5种LLM、RAG参数和操作约束，共记录105种配置、1,949次运行和49,109次RAG检索。结果显示基准可用于资格认证、回归测试和直接比较，但无法可靠预测代理在新任务上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理正被用于控制科学表征工具（如显微镜和同步辐射束线），但代理系统工程的成熟范式尚少。设计显微镜代理时面临大量选择（LLM型号、代理数量、职责分配、RAG参数等），研究者不仅需要代理能正确执行已知任务，还需要其能泛化到未见过的任务。缺乏系统的基准测试框架来评估这些设计决策的影响。

Method: 构建了一个基准测试与追踪日志框架，包含53个显微基准测试，评估了：1) 单代理、双代理、三代理图拓扑结构；2) 5种不同LLM；3) 不同的RAG和上下文参数；4) 操作约束条件。共系统记录了105种代理配置、1,949次独立测试运行和49,109次RAG检索，并在这些数据上训练surrogate模型以测试其对新任务的预测能力。

Result: 直接对比显示不同配置在延迟、token使用量、成本和失败模式上存在显著差异。然而，基于代理架构和测试结果训练的surrogate模型无法可靠预测代理在未见任务上的表现。表明当前异构测试套件不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 当前的基准测试框架对于代理的资格认证、回归测试、故障诊断和直接配置比较是有用的工具，但现有的异构测试集不足以支撑一个全局最优配置的预测模型。由于无法从基准性能可靠外推到新任务的泛化能力，未来需要开发更结构化的基准设计来支持任务无关的代理性能预测。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [130] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个基于ARACNe调控网络预测基因扰动下游转录效应的代理框架，通过MCP接口暴露。作者使用TCGA患者肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低的反向代理来验证预测方向，而不仅仅是预测基因的成员资格。MYC靶标预测在三类癌症中表现出高一致性（BRCA 90.0%、COAD 72.0%、STAD 85.7%），且在独立队列中复制。但相比MSigDB基线，其准确性未显著超越现有生物知识，且验证结果呈基因特异性：增殖机制调控因子大多成功，而谱系身份转录因子和CCND2持续失败。LLM代理在MCP工具调用接地方面达到71.4%-85.7%的精确匹配率，但两个模型都在模糊查询上自信地默认错误扰动类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对基因扰动预测工具的验证仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因（成员资格验证），而忽略了预测方向是否正确。作者提出需要使用真实患者肿瘤数据来验证预测的方向性，即被预测为上调的基因在扩增肿瘤中确实上调，反之亦然。

Method: 使用TCGA患者肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为剂量代理，模拟基因敲低的逆过程。对MYC、E2F及15个额外基因进行验证，使用PAM50亚型对照和METABRIC独立队列复制。同时使用Fisher精确检验与MSigDB基因集基线比较，并对LLM代理在MCP工具调用接地性能进行单独基准测试（35个查询）。

Result: MYC预测靶标与扩增肿瘤表达高度一致（BRCA 90.0%、COAD 72.0%、STAD 85.7%，p<0.0013），显著高于置换基线，通过PAM50对照并在METABRIC中复制（87.2%）。但与MSigDB基线相比，CASCADE并未显示超越现有MYC/E2F生物学知识。扩展验证显示基因特异性：增殖机制基因多成功，谱系因子和CCND2失败。LLM代理接地率为71.4%（小模型）和85.7%（大模型），两者在模糊查询上均自信地选择错误扰动类型。

Conclusion: 方向性验证（而非成员资格验证）是更严格的评估范式，CASCADE在增殖相关基因上表现良好，但在谱系身份因子上泛化不足。LLM代理的工具接地存在稳健性问题，特别是模糊查询下的错误默认行为难以通过现有修复策略解决。研究揭示了基于已知生物学知识基线（MSigDB）与数据驱动方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [131] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用GPT-3.5大语言模型进行反事实分析，聚焦于在线借贷场景中的投资回报率（ROI）预测。研究发现提示工程可显著提升GPT的预测性能（R²从1.97%提升至2.84%），接近梯度提升回归的3.48%；同时GPT在生成反事实ROI时展现出逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对于决策制定至关重要，尤其在评估不同利率方案的投资回报时。作者希望探索大语言模型（GPT-3.5）在该领域的应用潜力，检验其预测性能与因果推理能力，从而拓展LLM在预测和决策场景中的适用范围。

Method: 以在线借贷为研究背景，首先对比GPT-3.5与先进机器学习算法（如梯度提升回归）的预测性能，采用提示工程（prompt engineering）优化GPT的预测输出；随后利用GPT在多个替代利率下生成反事实ROI，并评估其逻辑一致性与因果推理质量。

Result: 提示工程将GPT的R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%；GPT在生成不同利率方案下的反事实ROI时表现出一致的逻辑推理能力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在反事实分析中展现出可行性与潜力，特别是在在线借贷场景中，有望成为传统机器学习方法的有效补充或替代工具，可推广至更广泛的预测与决策任务中。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [132] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoctorAgents是一个基于智能体（Agent）的人工智能框架，通过多个专门的LLM智能体（生成、验证、精炼）协同工作，以推理驱动的文本梯度下降替代穷举搜索，自动构建和优化端到端机器学习流水线，用于小规模、异质且时序复杂的临床数据。在多种临床任务上，该框架超越了现有AutoML基准，并生成更具可解释性的任务特定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习在高风险医疗决策中具有巨大潜力，但其可靠部署受限于数据稀缺、异质性和时序复杂性。现有AutoML系统主要依赖在预定义空间中进行的穷举式暴力搜索，缺乏显式推理能力和记忆机制，导致开发有效ML流水线既耗时又易出错。因此需要一种能从穷举搜索转向推理驱动的精炼方法。

Method: 提出DoctorAgents框架，采用多个专门的LLM智能体（生成、验证、精炼）协作，以自主构建和优化端到端ML流水线。核心创新在于通过文本梯度下降（textual gradient descent）反向传播自然语言反馈，实现针对性更新，无需穷举搜索。

Result: 在多种临床任务上的实验表明，DoctorAgents一致性地优于已建立的AutoML基线方法，同时生成更具可解释性的任务特定表示。

Conclusion: 将AutoML从穷举搜索改革为推理驱动的精炼是处理小规模临床数据的有效范式，DoctorAgents展示了基于智能体的、具备推理和记忆能力的AI框架在临床ML流水线自动化中的潜力。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [133] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C³PO基准，包含3,404个跨视频、音频、图像和文本的样本，评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在信息组合和反事实冲突两种能力上的表现。研究发现即使最优模型（Gemini-3.1-Pro）仅有73.17%的准确率（人类为88.64%），86-95%的失败源于模态主导问题，模型过度集中于文本模态而忽略矛盾证据，中间层注意力熵可预测推理正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型虽然能处理多种感官输入，但其推理仍然严重偏向主导模态，导致跨模态推理脆弱。需要系统性的基准和诊断方法来定位跨模态推理失败的原因。

Method: 构建C³PO基准，包含成对的IC（信息组合）和CC（反事实冲突）结构及四层设计，通过25个逻辑模板的全自动流水线生成3,404个样本。使用注意力探针分析模型内部注意力分配和中间层注意力熵来诊断失败机制。

Result: 人类准确率为88.64%，最优模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅达73.17%，开源模型在冲突场景下表现崩溃。86-95%的失败源于模态主导，模型将87-95%的注意力集中于文本。中间层注意力熵可预测正确性，且56个百分点的准确率差距表明性能取决于模态在冲突解决中的结构角色而非组合方式。

Conclusion: 多模态感知并不保证鲁棒的推理能力；架构必须支持持续的跨模态注意力，以避免过早收敛到单一模态而导致的推理失败。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions). C$^3$PO's paired IC/CC structure and four-tier design enable targeted diagnosis of when and why cross-modal reasoning fails. Built through a fully automatic pipeline using 25 logically grounded templates, C$^3$PO reveals that while humans achieve 88.64% accuracy, the best model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) reaches only 73.17%, with open-source models collapsing under conflict. Through attention probes, we find 86-95% of failures stem from modality dominance: models commit to one modality while ignoring contradictory evidence, concentrating 87-95% of attention on text. Mid-layer attention entropy predicts correctness-sustained exploration succeeds, premature collapse fails. The 56-point accuracy gap between equally complex templates reveals that performance depends on modalities' structural roles in conflict resolution, not combinations. These findings show multimodal perception does not guarantee robust reasoning; architectures must enable sustained cross-modal attention to avoid premature

</details>


### [134] [Adaptive Arena-based Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts for Open-Ended Care Plan Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05391)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,René Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANOE是一个用于护理计划协调的多智能体神经符号框架，通过五大模块（复杂度评估、自适应团队招募、基于竞技场的定量双极论证框架的角色论证计算、人在环路的争议机制、护理计划综合）实现透明且可争议的护理计划生成，在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG数据集上验证了临床正确性、安全性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
